# Catalog skill review — 2026-09-25

Status: complete. Scope: all 280 catalog skills. Resumed 2026-09-27. At most 20 reviewers run concurrently; skill-engineering is reviewed last.

Progress: pending 0; running 0; implemented 0; validated 280; blocked 0.

Pre-existing user work preserved, including docs/agent-harness-optimization-plan.md and the earlier partial catalog review. Starting state and attribution are recorded in the JSON record.

Status meanings: pending = not started; running = reviewer owns package; implemented = reviewed and local work finished, awaiting global validation; validated = required integration checks passed; blocked = concrete unresolved issue. A completed review may justify no content changes.

Global validation: npm run registry:build passed after nine optional handoff declarations were added to seven manifests; generated 280 skills. npm run verify passed: build, architecture boundaries (7 packages), lint, full format check, registry consistency (280), version integrity (17 unchanged published versions checked; 263 bumped packages), and 355 tests passed, 0 failed, 0 skipped. Full log: C:/Users/robso/AppData/Local/Temp/skill-review-resume-L4rvro/verify.log. git diff --check passed..

Behavioral evidence: 18 advice-only actor runs across six cases: nine prior runs preserved, plus nine resumed original/revised/no-skill runs across credential routing, temporal authority and worker/permit dependencies. Every resumed answer met its frozen criteria, so no comparative gain was observed in that subset. Original earlier results and limitations remain separate. Exact prompts, raw answers, rubric assessments and snapshot identities are in catalog-review-2026-09-25-evaluation.json; procedural isolation, single runs and teaching overlap limit inference. Technical fixture results and structured walkthroughs are not agent behavior measurements.

Details, domain criteria, findings, validation, versions, files and limitations: [JSON record](catalog-review-2026-09-25.json).

Individual handoffs preserve their authoring-time checks and limitations. Their historical pending-global notes are superseded by the final global validation above; unexecuted domain integration checks remain unexecuted.

Review criteria: API and language skills: consumer contracts, invalid states, compatibility and observable failure behavior. Concurrency and distributed skills: ownership, ordering, cancellation, progress, consistency and bounded resource lifetimes. Performance and runtime skills: representative work, attributable mechanisms, valid measurements, version-specific behavior and explicit limits. Architecture and discovery skills: stakeholder constraints, meaningful alternatives, proportional evidence and justified preservation of adequate solutions. Agent instruction skills: activation boundaries, authority, consequential decision rules, routed resources and verifiable outcomes without importing the review workflow.

| Decision or failure mode                  | Previous guidance                                                                         | Revised guidance                                                                                        | Evidence and limits                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| ----------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Timeout of one waiter sharing work        | Cancellation ownership remained implicit for coalesced CompletableFuture results.         | Isolate each waiter’s completion view; retain an owner and bounded lifetime for shared work.            | timeouts-and-deadlines: 61 assertions on Temurin25 with Java11 API compilation; no remote-resource cancellation guarantee.                                                                                  |
| JDK24+ monitor pinning                    | Version-only statements could rule out monitor pinning in legacy locking mode.            | Check effective locking mode; default and legacy configurations receive different diagnosis and remedy. | virtual-threads-internals: same synchronized-sleep fixture emitted 0 events with default locking and 2 with legacy mode on Temurin25.0.3; source verified for HotSpot24 GA, no universal event-count claim. |
| Complete serialized message               | Framing and exact payload completion were not operationalized.                            | Establish the boundary owner, bounded declared length, actual received bytes and message count.         | serialization-performance: 13 Java framing checks; protobuf-java4.36.2 accepted a truncated declared frame in the supplied fixture. No throughput improvement measured.                                     |
| Bearer credential transport               | Token validity guidance lacked query/form transport regression checks.                    | Keep header-only authentication explicit, verify rejected transports and zero handler effects.          | spring-security-for-apis: preserved27 cases and expanded29-case fixture passed; two temporary unsafe resolver mutations failed the added assertions. TLS/proxy behavior not exercised.                      |
| Full HTML versus fragment cache selection | Vary:Accept guidance did not distinguish same-media-type header-selected representations. | Vary on the actual selector or use separate URLs/nonreuse, and check history/validator semantics.       | view-and-representation-patterns: four Edge cache fixtures reproduced and prevented wrong-variant reuse. Actual htmx history/sanitizer runtime was not exercised.                                           |

Useful examples, caveats, technology baselines and specialist responsibilities were retained or replaced with reasons recorded per package. Reviews with no supported defect preserved their packages. These technical checks support specific corrected contracts; they do not measure a catalog-wide improvement in agent behavior.
